Run with: `node script.mjs` (requires `pptxgenjs` installed).

## Exports from masters.js

| Export | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `createPresentation(title?)` | `async function` | Returns PptxGenJS instance with masters registered and layout set |
| `registerMasters(pptx)` | `function` | Register masters on an existing PptxGenJS instance |
| `THEME` | `object` | Semantic color aliases: `text`, `background`, `brand`, `accent2`–`accent6`, `footer` |
| `THEME_COLORS` | `object` | Raw theme slots: `dk1`, `lt1`, `dk2`, `lt2`, `accent1`–`accent6`, `hlink`, `folHlink` |
| `PALETTE` | `object` | Extended palette with tint/shade variants per color slot |
| `CHART_COLORS` | `string[]` | Accent hex values ordered for chart data series |
| `FONT` | `string` | Primary font name (heading font) |
| `HEADING_FONT` | `string` | Heading font name |
| `BODY_FONT` | `string` | Body font name |
| `POS` | `object` | Pre-calculated content positioning areas |

## Positioning (`POS`)

Pre-calculated content areas. Spread into PptxGenJS options: `{ ...POS.body, chartColors: CHART_COLORS }`

| Key | x | y | w | h | Notes |
|-----|---|---|---|---|-------|
| `full` | 0 | 0 | 26.67 | 15 | Full slide |
| `title` | 2.96 | 2.07 | 20.75 | 1.92 | Title area |
| `body` | 0.89 | 2.4 | 24.95 | 10.81 | Content area below title+subtitle |
| `subtitle` | 0.89 | 13.06 | 12.44 | 0.65 | Subtitle area |
| `coverTitle` | 5.09 | 2.47 | 16.55 | 3.47 | Cover slide title |
| `coverSubtitle` | 19.26 | 13.97 | 6.54 | 0.61 | Cover slide subtitle |
| `dividerText` | 15.27 | -0.22 | 12.95 | 15.83 | Divider section title |

## Theme Colors

### THEME (semantic aliases)

- `THEME.text` = `#000000` (default text)
- `THEME.background` = `#FFFFFF` (default background)
- `THEME.brand` = `#0092F4` (primary brand accent)
- `THEME.accent2` = `#20DC86`
- `THEME.accent3` = `#DFD902`
- `THEME.accent4` = `#F7931E`
- `THEME.accent5` = `#FF4800`
- `THEME.accent6` = `#DA54D8`

### THEME_COLORS (raw slots)

- Dark 1 (text): `#000000`
- Light 1 (background): `#FFFFFF`
- Dark 2: `#000000`
- Light 2: `#B1B1B0`
- Accent 1 (primary): `#0092F4`
- Accent 2: `#20DC86`
- Accent 3: `#DFD902`
- Accent 4: `#F7931E`
- Accent 5: `#FF4800`
- Accent 6: `#DA54D8`
- Hyperlink: `#0563C1`
- Followed Hyperlink: `#954F72`

### CHART_COLORS

Accent colors ordered for data visualization: `slide.addChart(type, data, { chartColors: CHART_COLORS })`

1. `#0092F4` (accent1)
2. `#20DC86` (accent2)
3. `#DFD902` (accent3)
4. `#F7931E` (accent4)
5. `#FF4800` (accent5)
6. `#DA54D8` (accent6)

### Extended Palette (`PALETTE`)

Each theme color has tint/shade variants matching PowerPoint's color picker:

```js
PALETTE.accent1.base       // base color
PALETTE.accent1.lighter80  // 80% lighter (near white)
PALETTE.accent1.lighter60  // 60% lighter
PALETTE.accent1.lighter40  // 40% lighter
PALETTE.accent1.lighter25  // 25% lighter
PALETTE.accent1.darker25   // 25% darker
PALETTE.accent1.darker50   // 50% darker (near black)
```

Example (accent1 `#0092F4`): lighter80=`#CAEAFF`, lighter40=`#5FBFFF`, darker25=`#006DB7`, darker50=`#00497A`

Available slots: `dk1`, `lt1`, `dk2`, `lt2`, `accent1`–`accent6`, `hlink`, `folHlink`. See `theme.json` for all computed values.

## Theme Fonts

- Headings: Avenir Next LT Pro Demi
- Body: Avenir Next LT Pro
- `FONT` export = `'Avenir Next LT Pro Demi'`

### Multi-Column Layouts

Column placeholders share the same name but differ by x position. PptxGenJS fills by name (first match), so use **manual positioning** for multi-column content:

```js
// SPEAKER_INTRO_DARK_A — 4 columns:
const col1 = { x: 3.02, y: 4.49, w: 4.89, h: 6.5 };
const col2 = { x: 8.29, y: 4.49, w: 4.89, h: 6.5 };
const col3 = { x: 13.56, y: 4.49, w: 4.89, h: 6.5 };
const col4 = { x: 18.83, y: 4.49, w: 4.89, h: 6.5 };
slide.addText(content, { ...col1, fontFace: FONT, fontSize: 11, valign: 'top' });

// SPEAKER_INTRO_DARK_B — 5 columns:

const col2 = { x: 5.92, y: 4.49, w: 4.8, h: 6.5 };
```

## Typography

Font: `Avenir Next LT Pro Demi` (use `FONT` constant). Always specify `fontFace: FONT` on every `addText()`, `addTable()`, and `addChart()` call.

Recommended sizes for manual content (placeholders handle their own sizing):

| Element | Size | Weight | Color |
|---------|------|--------|-------|
| Section header (in body) | 14–16pt | Bold | `THEME.brand` |
| Body text | 11–12pt | Regular | `THEME.text` |
| Table header row | 10–11pt | Bold | `THEME.background` (on colored bg) |
| Table body | 9–10pt | Regular | `THEME.text` |
| Callout / highlight | 11–12pt | Bold | `THEME.accent2` or `THEME.brand` |
| Footnote / source | 7–8pt | Regular | `THEME.footer` or gray |
| Chart axis labels | 9–10pt | Regular | `THEME.text` |

## Content Best Practices

### Design Approach

**Content first, then layout.** Decide what you need to communicate, then pick the master that fits:
- Choose layouts based on content structure — let the material guide the design
- Dense data and analysis → full-width layouts (TITLE_ONLY or single-column) for maximum space
- Side-by-side comparisons or parallel concepts → multi-column layouts
- Emphasis or transition → subtitle or divider layouts

**CRITICAL: Fill the content area.** Use `POS.body` for positioning:
- `POS.body` — 24.95" × 10.81" — content area below title

Spread `{ ...POS.body }` into every content element.

**Content capacity** (~10.81" tall):
- Table at 10pt: ~33 rows | Table at 9pt: ~38 rows
- Text at 11pt: ~49 lines | Text at 9pt: ~60 lines
- Chart: fills area automatically

### Tables

```js
slide.addTable(rows, {
  ...POS.body,
  fontFace: FONT, fontSize: 10, color: THEME.text,
  border: { type: 'solid', pt: 0.5, color: 'E0E0E0' },
  autoPage: true,
});
// Header row: { fill: { color: THEME.brand }, color: THEME.background, bold: true }
// Alt rows:   { fill: { color: PALETTE.accent1.lighter80 } }
```

### Charts

```js
slide.addChart(pres.charts.BAR, chartData, {
  ...POS.body, chartColors: CHART_COLORS,
  showValue: true, valueFontSize: 9,
  catAxisLabelFontSize: 10, catAxisLabelFontFace: FONT,
  valAxisLabelFontFace: FONT,
});
```

## PptxGenJS API Patterns

### Text with Bullets

Use a **flat array** with `bullet: true` per item. Do NOT use nested arrays — they produce corrupt files.

```js
// CORRECT: flat array, bullet per paragraph
slide.addText([
  { text: 'First point', options: { bullet: true, fontSize: 10, fontFace: FONT } },
  { text: 'Second point', options: { bullet: true, fontSize: 10, fontFace: FONT } },
], { x: 0.5, y: 1, w: 11, h: 3, valign: 'top' });

// CORRECT: bold header + normal text on same line
slide.addText([
  { text: 'Header: ', options: { bold: true, bullet: true, fontSize: 10, fontFace: FONT, color: THEME.brand } },
  { text: 'Normal text continues here', options: { fontSize: 10, fontFace: FONT } },
  { text: 'Next header: ', options: { bold: true, bullet: true, fontSize: 10, fontFace: FONT, color: THEME.brand, breakType: 'break' } },
  { text: 'More text', options: { fontSize: 10, fontFace: FONT } },
], { x: 0.5, y: 1, w: 11, h: 3, valign: 'top' });

// WRONG: nested arrays produce corrupt PPTX files
// slide.addText([[{text: 'a'}, {text: 'b'}]], { bullet: true }); // DON'T DO THIS
```

### Sizing and Overflow

- PptxGenJS does **not** auto-shrink text to fit. If text overflows the box, it clips or spills.
- Tables do **not** auto-expand rows. Set `rowH` explicitly or let PptxGenJS calculate (omit `rowH`).
- When placing multiple elements vertically, **calculate y positions manually**: `nextY = prevY + prevH + gap`.
- Body area: 24.95" × 10.81" starting at (0.89, 2.4). Plan your layout to fill this rectangle.

### Shapes

```js
// Colored box (KPI card, callout, section divider)
slide.addShape(pres.shapes.ROUNDED_RECTANGLE, {
  x: 0.5, y: 1, w: 3, h: 0.6, fill: { color: PALETTE.accent1.lighter80 }, rectRadius: 0.05,
});
// Horizontal rule
slide.addShape(pres.shapes.LINE, { x: 0.5, y: 4, w: 11.5, h: 0, line: { color: 'D0D0D0', width: 0.5 } });
```

## Key Conventions

- **CRITICAL: `fontFace: FONT`** on EVERY `addText()` / `addTable()` / `addChart()` call. PptxGenJS does not cascade fonts from masters.
- **CRITICAL: `{ ...POS.body }`** on content elements. This ensures content fills the available space.
- **Footers and logos are automatic.** Masters include copyright, slide numbers, and branding. Do not add these manually.
- **Placeholders** handle font, size, color, and position: `slide.addText(text, { placeholder: 'Name' })`.
- **Suppress unused placeholders:** Fill any placeholder you don't need with a space: `slide.addText(' ', { placeholder: 'Name' })`. This prevents "Click to add..." prompts and empty boxes in exports.
- **Slide dimensions:** 26.6667" × 15".
- **Color usage:** `THEME.text` for body text, `THEME.brand` for emphasis/headers, accents for differentiation, `PALETTE.accent1.lighter80` for alternating table rows, `PALETTE.accent1.lighter60` for subtle backgrounds.

## Common Pitfalls

- **Text overflow**: PptxGenJS does not auto-shrink text. If content exceeds the text box, it silently clips or overflows. Split long bullet lists into columns or across slides.
- **Empty space**: If the bottom third of your slide is unused, the slide looks unfinished. Distribute content across the full body area or choose a smaller layout.
- **Color contrast**: Dark text on dark backgrounds (or light text on light backgrounds) is unreadable. Check the Available Masters table for each layout's background color and choose text colors accordingly.
- **Layout monotony**: Using the same master layout repeatedly makes the deck feel generic. This template has 146 layouts — vary them.
- **Missing fontFace**: PptxGenJS does not cascade fonts from masters. Every `addText()`, `addTable()`, `addChart()` call needs `fontFace: FONT` or text renders in Calibri.
- **Table column overflow**: Column widths must sum to the container width. Mismatched widths cause silent overflow.
- **Concatenated text blocks**: Build each logical section as a separate `addText()` call with its own positioning, not as one giant concatenated string.
- **Line shapes with negative dimensions**: `pres.shapes.LINE` with negative `w` or `h` (e.g., an upward-sloping line) can corrupt the PPTX file, causing PowerPoint to require repair on open. For complex visuals like trendlines, use SVG-as-image instead (see Workflow step 3).
- **Hex color format**: PptxGenJS only accepts 6-digit hex without `#` (e.g., `"1A3C6E"`). Alpha/opacity suffixes like `"FFFFFF70"` throw errors. Use solid colors only.
- **`lineSpacingMultiple` overrides**: Avoid overriding line spacing unless absolutely necessary. Custom values interact unpredictably with different font sizes and narrow containers, making overflow bugs hard to diagnose. Default spacing (1.0) is safest.

## Workflow

### 1. Design with HTML preview

Create an HTML file with one `<div>` per slide to prototype layout and spacing before writing PptxGenJS code:

```html
<div style="width:26.6667in; height:15in; border:1px solid #ccc; position:relative; font-family:Avenir Next LT Pro Demi,sans-serif; overflow:hidden;">
  <!-- Title bar -->
  <div style="position:absolute; left:0.5in; top:0.38in; width:12.3in; height:0.37in; font-size:21pt;">Title</div>
  <div style="position:absolute; left:0.89in; top:2.4in; width:24.95in; height:10.81in; border:1px dashed #0092F4;">
    <!-- Your content layout here: tables, charts, text blocks -->
  </div>
</div>
```

Use CSS px for font sizes and positioning in the HTML — these get converted to pt and inches in step 3. The HTML preview uses the browser's text engine for rapid iteration; PPTX rendering differs, so step 4 catches mismatches.

### 2. Visually inspect HTML

Render the HTML preview and **look at it**. Check every slide for visual quality — does content fill the body area? Is text readable? Is the layout balanced? Fix in HTML until every slide looks right. Iterating here is much faster than re-generating PPTX.

### 3. Translate to PptxGenJS

Convert the finalized HTML layouts to `addText()`, `addTable()`, `addChart()`, `addShape()` calls. Key conversions:

**Positions and sizes:**
- CSS `px` to inches: divide by 96 (`px / 96`)
- CSS `%` widths: calculate as fraction of parent width in inches
- Flexbox/grid layouts: replace with calculated absolute `x, y, w, h` positions
- CSS borders: replace with thin rectangles (`w: 0.01` for 1px lines)
- CSS backgrounds: add filled rectangles **before** content (z-order = insertion order)

**Font sizes (px to pt):**
- Use **0.85x** as the base conversion: `pt = px × 0.85`, rounded to nearest 0.5pt
- Browsers and PowerPoint render text differently — 1:1 conversion overflows, 0.75x looks too small
- For narrow containers (sidebars, margin labels): reduce to 0.75x
- Leave 10-15% extra width in text boxes vs the HTML layout to account for different text wrapping

**Charts:**
- Simple data charts: use `addChart()` with `CHART_COLORS`
- Custom SVG charts (gradients, trendlines, annotations): screenshot the SVG as a PNG at 2x resolution, then embed with `addImage({ data: "image/png;base64,..." })`
- Do NOT build complex charts from shape primitives — line shapes with negative dimensions corrupt the PPTX file

### 4. Render PPTX and visually verify

```bash
node slides.mjs                                      # Generate PPTX
soffice --headless --convert-to pdf output.pptx       # Convert to PDF
pdftoppm -png -r 150 output.pdf slide                 # Split into per-slide PNGs
```

**View every slide PNG.** Look for:
- Text clipping or overflow (content cut off at edges)
- Large empty areas (unused lower half of slide)
- Unreadable text (wrong color on background, too small)
- Visual monotony (same layout repeated)
- Missing fonts (text appears in default Calibri instead of template font)

Fix issues and re-render until every slide looks right.

## Style Guide

If a `STYLE_GUIDE.md` file exists in this directory, read it before building slides. It contains user-specific design preferences (dos/don'ts, typography, color usage, layout preferences) that override or supplement the defaults in this document.
